    WebAutomation.ExecuteJavascript BrowserInstance: edgeBrowser Javascript: $'''function Claim_CPT_DT() {
    var table = document.querySelector(\'table[id^=\"claimCPTCodesTable\"]\');
    var tableData = [];

    if (table) {
        var headers = [];

        // Extract headers from the first row
        var headerCells = table.rows[0].cells;
        for (var i = 0; i < headerCells.length; i++) {
            var headerText = headerCells[i].innerText.trim();
            headers.push(headerText || `Column_${i}`);
        }

        // Loop through the rest of the rows
        for (var i = 1; i < table.rows.length; i++) {
            var rowData = {};
            var cells = table.rows[i].cells;

            for (var j = 0; j < cells.length; j++) {
                var cell = cells[j];
                var cellText = \'\';

                var inputElement = cell.querySelector(\'input\');
                if (inputElement) {
                    cellText = inputElement.value.trim();
                } else {
                    cellText = cell.innerText.trim();
                }

                rowData[headers[j]] = cellText;

                // Extract title attribute from column index 2
                if (j === 2) {
                    rowData[\"Code Title\"] = cell.getAttribute(\"title\") || \"\";
                }
            }

            tableData.push(rowData);
        }
    }

    // Initialize analysis objects
    const DI_Info = {
        IsDI_CPTPresent: false,
        IsMultiple: false,
        DI_cpt: [],
        CombinedCpt: \"\",
        CombinedTitle: \"\"
    };

    const NDC_Info = {
        IsNDCCodePresent: false,
        NDC_cpt: []
    };

    const Bill_Fee = {
        IsBillFeeZero: false,
        Bill_Fee_CPT: []
    };

    // Analyze extracted data
    let diMatches = 0;

    tableData.forEach(row => {
        const code = row[\"Code\"];
        const codeTitle = row[\"Code Title\"];
        const billedFee = row[\"Billed Fee\"];

        // DI CPT logic
        if ([\"93306\", \"93307\", \"93308\", \"93320\", \"93351\"].includes(code) || code.startsWith(\"7\")) {
            DI_Info.IsDI_CPTPresent = true;
            DI_Info.DI_cpt.push([code, codeTitle]);
            diMatches++;
        }

        // NDC logic
        if (code.startsWith(\"J\") || code.startsWith(\"90\")) {
            NDC_Info.IsNDCCodePresent = true;
            NDC_Info.NDC_cpt.push(code);
        }

        // Billed Fee logic
        if (billedFee === \"0.00\") {
            Bill_Fee.IsBillFeeZero = true;
            if (!(code.startsWith(\"7\") || code.startsWith(\"90\"))) {
                Bill_Fee.Bill_Fee_CPT.push(code);
            }
        }
    });

    DI_Info.IsMultiple = diMatches > 1;

    // Add Combinedcpt and CombinedTitle inside DI_Info
    if (DI_Info.IsDI_CPTPresent && DI_Info.DI_cpt.length > 0) {
        DI_Info.CombinedCpt = DI_Info.DI_cpt.map(item => item[0]).join(\'\\n\');
        DI_Info.CombinedTitle = DI_Info.DI_cpt.map(item => item[1]).join(\'\\n\');
    }

    // Final result object
    const result = {
        tableData,
        DI_Info,
        NDC_Info,
        Bill_Fee
    };

    // Return stringified JSON
    return JSON.stringify(result);
}
